**Exit-rule measurement — build K → build 2026-06-05-L (SHADOW exit comparison):**
- **Motivation:** the first live GOLD win closed at the fixed `ProfitTargetUSD=$100` while the M5 downtrend ran another ~130 pts (user chart). Fixed $ target caps winners in trends. User asked what logic fixes it AND flagged that **silver/oil behave differently — exit rules must adapt to the symbol.**
- **Discipline (not bolting it on from one chart):** measure fixed vs trailing exit across ALL setups via the triple-barrier shadow before changing live. This is the "asymmetric barriers (lift avg R)" AFML step.
- **EA build L (SHADOW path only — TRADE/live behaviour untouched):** `CheckPendingBarriers()` now tracks, per signal, BOTH the fixed-barrier outcome (unchanged `barrier_hit`/`r_multiple`) AND a simulated trailing exit (activate at `TrailActivateR=1.0`, trail `TrailDistanceR=0.5` behind peak, same −1R adverse stop), plus MFE. Three new CSV columns: `mfe_r`, `trail_hit`, `trail_r` (setups DB now 14 cols; `LookupSetupStats` cell-skip updated; live `LookupSizing` path unaffected — reads separate sizing CSV).
- **Built `gridstat_exitcompare.py`:** per-setup fixed vs trailing total R + an MFE-based trail-distance sweep to find each symbol's optimal give-back. Takes a symbol arg (gold/silver/oil) → reads that symbol's own setups file.
- **SYMBOL-ADAPTIVE BY DESIGN (answers user's concern):** all exits are in R = move/(ATR_D1×BarrierATR). Because ATR is symbol-specific, an R-distance trail **auto-scales to each instrument's volatility** — same `TrailDistanceR` is a wider $ trail on GOLD than SILVER. The mechanism adapts automatically; the optimal trail DISTANCE (trend persistence differs: GOLD trends hard, SILVER/OIL may chop) is **measured per symbol** via the sweep, never hardcoded/copied. Each symbol: own shadow → own exitcompare → own exit choice.
- **RESULT (2026-06-05 GOLD shadow, 229 signals):** TRAILING beats fixed decisively — **total +20.65R (trailing 0.5R) vs +12.85R (fixed) = +61%.** Both accurate EA-log and MFE model agree (deploy condition met). 8 setups favor trail, 4 tie, 3 favor fixed. Biggest gains in trending/EXP setups (BUY|HR_NY|ATR_EXP +3.20R). Trail-distance sweep: **tighter is better for GOLD** — 0.30R best (+22.44R model), degrades past 0.75R, negative at 1.0R+. NOTE 0.3R ≈ 24pt trail → whipsaw/spread risk live; re-run shadow at 0.3R & 0.4R for ACCURATE numbers + whipsaw check before locking. Caveat: a few setups (incl. live SELL|HR_OVL|ATR_NORM) preferred fixed — "trail wins overall" ≠ "everywhere". Shadow modeling-quality (intrabar high/low ordering) assumed favorable-first; treat absolutes as optimistic.
- **Sizing table rebuilt 2026-06-05** (`gridstat_rigor.py --write`) from FIXED outcomes — correct for the CURRENT live config (still fixed $100 exit). Now trades 8/15 setups.
- **Trail-distance accurate curve (each = its own shadow pass):** 0.3R +12.40R (LOSES to fixed — too tight, whipsaws: 30 TRAIL exits @ +0.94 avg); 0.5R +20.65R; 0.6R +20.33R. Plateau at 0.5–0.6R. **The MFE model said 0.3R optimal (+22.44) but accurate sim proved it a LOSER — model is path-blind, wildly optimistic for tight trails. Lesson: always confirm tight trails accurately.** Chose **0.6R** (tied with 0.5R on R; fewer trail exits = more robust to live spread).
- **CPCV on trailing (0.6R, `--trail`) — HUMBLING:** in-sample +0.347 R/trade (+40.21R) but OOS median +0.196 ≈ fixed's +0.195. **The +61% raw-backtest edge was largely in-sample/overfit; OOS trailing ≈ parity with fixed** (degradation 0.57× vs fixed 0.70×, 79% folds positive, verdict "EDGE LIKELY but soft").
- **DECISION: deploy trailing 0.6R anyway — for the STRUCTURAL reason, not the backtest.** `ProfitTargetUSD=$100` is a fixed dollar amount that never scales; as the account compounds and lots grow, $100 caps winners harder and harder (the chart problem worsens with success). An R-scaled trailing exit auto-scales with size and can't go stale. Expect near-parity now, growing edge as compounding kicks in. Proceed SMALL (LotSize 0.02), live journal is the arbiter.
- **DEPLOYED — EA build 2026-06-05-M (live TRADE path):** `UseTrailingExit=true` → in `ManageGrid()`, replaced the fixed `ProfitTargetUSD` close with an R-scaled trailing basket exit (activate `TrailActivateR=1.0`, give back `TrailDistanceR=0.6`; 1R$ = barrierDist × $/price/lot × open lots → auto-scales with size & symbol). `OpenSimpleSLTP` sets TP=0 when trailing (lets winners run; −1R SL unchanged). `g_peakBasketFloat` tracks the peak, resets when basket flat. Fixed-$ path retained as fallback (`UseTrailingExit=false`). Sizing table rebuilt from `trail_r` (gridstat_rigor.py `--trail --write`). Python tools gained `--trail` flag (load_signals `r_col`).
- **SILVER/OIL:** run their OWN exitcompare — optimal trail likely differs (choppier instruments may need wider). Never copy GOLD's 0.6R across.

### Session 14 — 2026-06-02

**Goal**: Trend EA (Marius Trend GOLD v1.0) failed to converge — flat results across Jan-Apr. User wants sophisticated approach combining hedged grid + statistical setup filtering.

**Why trend EA was abandoned (after 6 iterations of RealHedge tuning):**
- Same-lot hedge LOCKS IN loss at moment of hedging (e.g. -$200 baseline). Combined float never recovers because hedge cancels every directional move.
- Partial hedge (0.5x ratio) helps but each cycle exited at +$5 due to too-eager positiveExit threshold
- Patient trail (MinFloat=$200) rarely activated because combined peak barely reached $200
- April result oscillated -$300 to +$517 depending on parameter tweaks — that's noise, not edge
- Conclusion: hedge geometry on GOLD M5 is mathematically constrained; can't escape with parameter tweaks
- Snapshot saved at `ea\Marius Trend GOLD v1.0.mq4` (build 2026-06-02-F)

**User feedback that shifted direction:**
- "Basic trend follower with stops is beginner stuff. 20 years trading experience. Look at what professionals do."
- Rejected VWAP (doesn't work on GOLD)
- Chose: combine Hedged Grid (proper math) + Statistical Setup Filtering

**Created: `Marius GridStat GOLD v1.0.mq4`** (in MT4 Adapted folder + ea\ backup, 506 lines)

**Architecture:**
- **SHADOW mode** (StatsCollectionMode=true): no trades, every signal logged to CSV with triple-barrier outcomes
- **TRADE mode** (StatsCollectionMode=false, StatsFilterEnabled=true): lookup setup in stats DB, only trade if win_rate>=MinWinRate AND samples>=MinSamples
- **Triple-barrier method** (Lopez de Prado): upper barrier at +BarrierATR×D1ATR, lower barrier at -same, time barrier at TimeBarrierBars (288 = 24h on M5). Logs which hits first + R-multiple.
- Execution: simple SL/TP at ±0.8×ATR (mirrors stats methodology, no grid/hedge needed)

**Build progression:**
- A: initial design, profit target $100 + grid + hedge (Jan-only test, 20 samples too few)
- B: pure SHADOW mode, triple-barrier signals + no trades
- C: MAX_PENDING 500→5000 (buffer overflow)
- D: signal entries only on NEW BAR (Goldminer fires every tick = duplicate signals)
- E: BarrierATRMultiplier 1.5→0.8 (89.8% TIME exits too high)
- F: **CURRENT** — coarse fingerprint (DIRECTION | SESSION | ATR_REGIME), OpenSimpleSLTP() execution

**Stats results after build E (6 months, Dec 2025 - June 2026):**
- 226 signals, 65.5% decisive (UPPER/LOWER hits), 34.5% TIME

- **Session pattern:** BUY in NY profitable, BUY in LDN loses; SELL in LDN profitable, SELL in NY loses

### Session 13 — 2026-06-02

**Goal**: Split monolithic hedger into two focused EAs after Jan-Apr backtests revealed conflicting strategy goals.

**Backtest summary across 4 months** (current fib EA + all session 12 features):
- Jan: $3000 → ~$12k (windfall — accidental "freeze and hold" by IsInHedgePair bug captured 500-pt rally)
- Feb: $3000 → $40 (frozen 0.20 lot buys at 5075-5089 got 180-pt reversal → wipeout)
- Mar: $3000 → $4104 (sells fired into crash, partial recovery)
- Apr: $3000 → $445 (frozen 0.14 sell lots at 4600s got 218-pt rally → near wipeout)
- Net Jan-Apr ≈ +$4586, but ENTIRELY driven by Jan's lucky end-of-test alignment. Without Jan = -$4414.

**Diagnosis**: Current EA tries to do TWO opposite jobs at once:
- Scalping (BuySellProfitThreshold $30-50 harvests, $15 basket trail) -- closes positions before trends develop
- Trend-following (D1 filter, Fibonacci sizing) -- builds positions that scalping dismantles for tiny profits
The Jan windfall happened because IsInHedgePair bug accidentally disabled scalping and let trend logic ride.

**Decision**: Split into two separate EAs. Build trend EA first (more code reuse, clearer edge from Jan).

**Created: `Marius Trend GOLD v1.0.mq4`** (in MT4 Adapted folder + `ea\` backup)
- Branched from fib EA, scalping features turned OFF, trend features kept and extended.
- **Disabled inputs**: UseBuySellProfitThreshold=false, UseHarvestRetire=false, UseProactiveHedge=false, OrphanBlockLoss=9999
- **Widened exits**: MinFloatToActivate=200, BasketTrailAmount=150 (then ATR-scaled)
- **New: ATR basket trail** (`UseATRBasketTrail`, `ATRTrailMultiplier=2.0`) -- trail distance = ATR(D1) * multiplier * total open lots in $ terms. Gives GOLD breathing room (D1 ATR ≈ 60-100 points × 2 × 0.10 lots × 100 = $1200-$2000 trail)
- **New: RealHedge** (`UseRealHedge=true`, `RealHedgeLossTrigger=200`) -- when basket float <= -$200, open SAME-SIZE opposite hedge to neutralize further loss. Replaces the placebo 0.02-vs-0.20 proactive hedge.
- **New: ReleaseRealHedge** -- when basket + hedge combined hits positive $5 OR trails off peak by ATR distance, close everything together
- **New: MaxBasketLots100** (default 30 = 0.30 max lots per direction) -- hard cap to prevent runaway Fibonacci
- **New: RealHedge active = block all new entries** -- basket is in repair mode, don't add complexity
- **Kept**: D1 trend filter (UseDailyTrendFilter=true), Goldminer+ADX+MAangle entry, Compounding, Session filter, Fibonacci sizing, MaxSameTrades=5

**Pending**:
- Compile and backtest the new trend EA on Jan-Apr GOLD M5 (same data)
- Compare to existing fib EA results (the +$4586 net was lucky -- trend EA should be more consistent)
- If trend EA shows real edge, build scalper EA as separate research project later
- The current fib EA stays untouched as a reference / fallback

### Session 12 — 2026-06-02

**Goal**: fix stuck orphan losers in GOLD fib EA after Jan backtest showed sells #8/#10 force-closed at -$2095/-$1872 (account blown).

**Iterations (most failed before landing on D1 filter):**
- Tried `TrendLockAngle` (block sells when MA angle > 60°) — too noisy, angle dipped during pullbacks
- Tried fast/slow EMA cross (20 vs 100) on M5 — flipped on intraday pullbacks, unlocked sells at wrong moments
- Tried intelligent depth cap (block deeper Fibonacci adds if direction in loss) — helped but didn't stop the first stuck position
- Tried hedge-protection in `BuySellProfitThreshold` (don't close profitable buys if sells losing) — caused heavier drawdowns when held positions moved further wrong
- Tried `RetireWorstLoser` with single-cycle budget — couldn't afford big stuck losers from single harvest
- Tried accumulated `g_retireBudgetSell` / `g_retireBudgetBuy` (carry leftover across harvests) — worked for small orphans (#10) but not for fast-growing ones; algorithm retired smaller losers first, never accumulated enough for the deep stuck position
- Tried "worst-first" retire policy (save budget if can't afford worst) — successfully retired #8, #10, #16, #17, #19, #28, #31, #34 with steady positive equity, BUT sell #36 grew to -$419 in 6 days during trend, eventually retired but bled equity
- Tried `OrphanBlockLoss` (block new same-direction entry if any existing position at <= -$50) — didn't prevent the FIRST counter-trend trade from being opened in a strong rally; Jan 20-28 still blew account ($1009 × 3 stuck sells)

**What finally worked — D1 trend filter (ported from SILVER variant):**
- Added inputs: `UseDailyTrendFilter=true`, `UseD1DirectionReset=false`, `DailyMA_Period=50`
- Added function `IsDailyTrendBullish()` — compares D1 EMA(50) bar-over-bar
- Added `CheckD1DirectionReset()` (called in OnTick before ManageGoldminerLogic) — closes opposite-direction positions when D1 flips (disabled by default)
- Added `d1Bull` / `d1Bear` gates before signal triggers — `if (ggreen > 0 && d1Bull)` and `if (gred > 0 && d1Bear)`

**Backtest result (Jan 2-29, GOLD M5, $3000 starting):**
- Final balance: **$4614** (+53.8% in 28 days)
- No orphans, no stuck baskets, no stop-outs
- During Jan 20-28 rally (4730 → 5230), only buys fired; sells never opened
- Compares to all prior approaches that blew the account

**Other features still in place (from earlier sessions, kept):**
- `RetireWorstLoser` with `UseHarvestRetire=true`, `HarvestRetireMinNet=10`, accumulated `g_retireBudget*` globals
- `OrphanBlockLoss=50` (still useful for non-trending periods when D1 is neutral)
- Intelligent depth cap removed (replaced by orphan-block)

**Pending:**
- Run full Jan-May backtest to verify D1 filter holds up across multiple trend regimes
- Test what happens when D1 flips during open positions (UseD1DirectionReset off vs on)
- Port back to SILVER variant if any of the new tweaks (RetireWorstLoser, OrphanBlockLoss) prove useful there

### Session 11 — 2026-05-30
- **All changes in `Marius Hedger M5 v1.0 fib.mq4`** (live MT4 folder + `ea\` backup kept in sync)

#### CheckUnwindPairs — bug fixes and extensions
- **Bug fix (critical)**: original `CheckUnwindPairs` paired the WORST (biggest) losing buy with best sell — the largest loser could never be covered; pair net was always negative so unwind never fired. Fixed to pair the OLDEST losing buy (age ≥ UnwindMinBars) with best profitable sell — oldest position is more likely to have a viable opposite-direction counterpart
- **Direction entry locks** added: `g_unwindBuyLock` / `g_unwindSellLock` — when old stuck buys exist, new BUY entries are blocked until all qualifying losers are cleared; prevents the EA from immediately refilling unwound slots with new Fibonacci positions at high levels
- **Hedge preservation flags** added: `g_preserveSells` / `g_preserveBuys` — when buy side total float < -2×MinFloatToActivate, blocks `BuySellProfitThreshold` from harvesting the profitable sells (and vice versa); preserves the hedge position needed for future unwinding
- **Trapped basket detection** added inside `CheckUnwindPairs`:
  - Detects when `|totalBuyLots - totalSellLots| ≤ LotSize` AND `combinedFloat < -MinFloatToActivate×3` AND oldest position ≥ `UnwindMinBars×5` (5 days)
  - When trapped: equal lots at different price levels = price-insensitive locked loss that the basket trail can NEVER clear
  - **Best-net pair search** (O(n²), max 25 combos): scans ALL buy-sell combinations to find the optimal pairing — not just oldest buy + best sell; finds pairs like buy118+sell115 that oldest-first logic would miss
  - **Dynamic threshold**: `trappedThreshold = combinedFloat - MinFloatToActivate` — hedge must provide at least MinFloatToActivate improvement over raw locked loss; scales with trap severity; prevents holding trapped positions indefinitely as swap costs accumulate
  - Logs as `TrappedUnwind(Buy/Sell)` — distinct from normal `UnwindPair` in journal

#### Adaptive MaxSameTrades
- When compounding is active (scale ≥ 2), `effectiveMaxTrades = ceil(MaxSameTrades / scale)` — prevents Fibonacci from going 5-deep with doubled lot sizes. At scale=2: max 3 positions instead of 5.

#### Orphan compensation — tried and removed
- **Added** (session 11 early): when `BuySellProfitThreshold` closed one direction creating orphans, next signal in opposite direction opened a matching lot to immediately rebalance the basket
- **Problem identified**: compensation lot size grew unboundedly — if new sells accumulated between the orphan and the compensation signal, the matching buy escalated to 0.30 lots on a $5,000 account ($30/point exposure). Caused 87.61% max drawdown and $44,046 gross loss vs $53,193 gross profit (PF 1.21)
- **Removed** (reverted to Option B): `OpenTrade` now calls `CalculateLotSize` directly with no imbalance scanning. Fibonacci sizing is disciplined; basket trail handles orphan situations naturally by closing both directions together when combined basket peaks
- **Decision**: pure basket trail architecture is cleaner — no compensation = no runaway lot escalation

#### Backtest results (Jan–May 2026 period)
- Without orphan compensation + with unwind/trapped basket: account grew $3,000 → ~$12,134 by Mar 2 before 4 end-of-test positions sat open until May 28. Trapped basket unwind dissolved the Jan 30 stuck pairs ($826 locked loss realised early vs $6,205 if held to May 28). Final balance ~$5,929 (end-of-test artefact — positions from Feb 10–12 sat 107 days accruing swap)
- With orphan compensation: $3,000 → $11,099 peak but PF 1.21, max drawdown 87.61%, gross loss $44,046 — deemed unsustainable

#### Pending
- Re-run optimisation: grisk (5→15 step 1), ADX_Threshold (15→35 step 5), MinFloatToActivate (40→120 step 10), BasketTrailAmount (10→30 step 5) — parameters may have shifted after orphan compensation removal
- Analyse optimisation results for PF ≥ 2.0 + max drawdown ≤ 25%
- Plan scalping EA: M1/M5, separate magic number, fixed $10–$20 profit target, no basket logic — runs alongside hedging EA on separate chart

### Session 10 — 2026-05-29
- **Catastrophic backtest identified**: 5-deep BUY stack opened 2026-01-14 at 4621–4637 sat open until end-of-test (4.5 months) with no exit — BasketTrail never fired (basket always in negative float); balance went from $4,036 to negative
- **Root cause**: no exit mechanism exists when basket stays in sustained loss. Hard stop loss rejected by user trading philosophy.
- **Fix — `CheckUnwindPairs()` (Layer 3 surgical unwinding)**: new function added to `Marius Hedger M5 v1.0 fib.mq4`
  - Runs once per new bar (not every tick)
  - Finds worst losing position in each direction + best profitable position in opposite direction
  - If worst loser has been open ≥ `UnwindMinBars` (default 288 bars = 24h on M5) AND pair net ≥ `UnwindMinNetGain` (default $10): closes both as a pair
  - One pair per bar — surgical, not a basket flush
  - Logs every closure: `UnwindPair: buy#X(-$Y) + sell#Z(+$W) net=$V barsOpen=N`
  - New inputs: `UseUnwindPairs=true`, `UnwindMinBars=288`, `UnwindMinNetGain=10.0`
- **Backup synced**: `ea\Marius Hedger M5 v1.0 fib.mq4`
- **Pending**: recompile in MT4, run backtest over Jan–May period to verify unwinding fires and prevents account blowup

### Session 6 — 2026-05-19–21
- **Fixed `ManageTradeClosures()` priority bug** in M5 adapted EA:
  - Root cause: individual sell-basket close (priority 3) was harvesting profitable SELLs for small gains ($64) while BUYs were -$142; combined close (priority 4) then ran but found no SELLs — hedge failed completely
  - Fix: restructured priority order — (1) combined net ≥ threshold → close ALL, (2) `if (UseCombinedClose && netProfit < 0) return` guard holds both open, (3) individual closes only fire when combined ≥ 0
  - Effect: profitable SELL hedge now stays open until it offsets the BUY loss, then both close together at a net gain

### Session 5 — 2026-05-18
- **EA v3.0** — all changes in `Adapted\Marius Hedger v1.0 adapted.mq4` (1295 lines):
  - **Cascade fix (CRITICAL)**: `ManageTrailingPendingOrders()` now filters `OrderComment() == CommentText` — only Layer 0 ("MyEA") market orders generate a Layer 1 pending. Hedge fills ("Entry - Hedger") and Layer 2 fills ("L2-Hedger") are silently ignored by the loop.
  - **Stop-level guard fixed**: `ManageSinglePendingOrder()` now uses `MathMax(brokerMin, GetATRAdjustedD() * Point)` as the minimum distance. When `MODE_STOPLEVEL=0` in backtester, this falls back to the ATR-D distance, making the guard actually work.
  - **ATR-dynamic trigger distance D**: New function `GetATRAdjustedD()` returns `clamp(ATR(14) × ATR_D_Multiplier / Point, 30, 500)`. Falls back to `PendingTrail` if ATR unavailable. Used in both `ManageTrailingPendingOrders()` and `ManageLayer2Pending()`.
  - **Layer 2 pending order**: New `ManageLayer2Pending()` + `PlaceOrUpdateLayer2Pending()` — after Layer 1 fills, places a recovery pending in the original direction at `Layer1Entry ± D`. Layer 2 lot = `Layer2LotMultiplier × LotSize` (default 3×). Comment = `"L2-Hedger"`.
  - **Python close signal polling**: New `CheckPythonCloseSignal()` — reads `close_signal.json` from MT4 Common Files each tick; if `close_all` + matching symbol found, calls `CloseAllBasketOrders()` and deletes the file.

├── edge_costs.py                    ← COST-FIRST edge hunt: enumerate MT5 universe by REAL round-trip spread(bps)+swap → _universe_costs.csv. Cheapest: US100 0.86 / US500 0.94 / US30 1.06 / GOLD 1.29 / EURGBP 2.78; oil 6.3-6.6 (2-leg=13bps). Run: `python edge_costs.py`.
├── edge_spread_screen.py            ← cointegration PAIRS net of real 2-leg cost. RESULT: DEAD (cheap pairs don't revert; reverting oil too expensive). Run: `python edge_spread_screen.py`.
├── edge_revert_screen.py            ← single-instrument variance-ratio reversion screen. KEY FINDING: GOLD VR≈1.0 (random walk!); EURGBP VR 0.67 (hardest reverter); FX majors 0.83-0.93. Run: `python edge_revert_screen.py`.
├── edge_eurgbp_bt.py                ← cost-aware single-leg z-reversion bt. RESULT: DEAD net of cost (PF 0.83 even EURGBP — naive z+stop has bad payoff). Run: `python edge_eurgbp_bt.py`.
├── edge_eurgbp_grid.py              ← grid (no-stop fade+scale-in, PT-harvest) sim. ⚠️ UNRELIABLE — failed the GOLD sanity (showed gold PF 0.19, but gold is proven PF 3.92) = bar grid-sim can't model floor/path ([[python-grid-sim-parity-wall]]). DON'T trust its numbers; only MT5 every-tick decides. Run: `python edge_eurgbp_grid.py`.
├── two_engine_lab.py                ← HARVESTER+TREND-RIDER pairing test (the book-level hedge). Reads fib C + GoldTrend per-bar FLOATING-equity logs (UseEquityLog=true) → combined floatDD, diversification ratio, daily-return correlation, + the CRUX: a LANDMINE-COVERAGE table (during each harvester drawdown, what did the trend-rider make?) + two_engine_overlay.png. Run: `python two_engine_lab.py [fibc_csv] [goldtrend_csv]` (defaults = Common\Files). Validated synthetic 37%→2.2% combined DD / corr −0.98. The test of "does the trend-rider cover the harvester's market-shift landmine."
├── portfolio_allocator.py           ← AFML Ch.16 portfolio layer: parses gold/silver/oil MT5 HTML reports → correlation → equal/inverse-var/HRP weights → combined FLOATING-DD + size-up to a DD budget. Run: `python portfolio_allocator.py [DDbudget%] [--write]`. RESULT 2026-06-18: streams ~uncorrelated; EQUAL-weight best (combined float DD ~9.4% vs gold-alone 20.2%). Reads *_report.html in project root.
├── gridstat_meta_xs.py              ← CROSS-SECTIONAL meta-labeling (Stage 1 of "ideal EA"/Path A). Pools ALL 19 scanner symbols at EACH symbol's best grisk (gold-anchor=4 ✓), purged K-fold + DSR head-to-head (fingerprint vs ML). RESULT 2026-06-19: RED — ML did NOT beat fingerprint (PSR 67.8%, both near-zero); 19 symbols did NOT rescue ML → bottleneck = FEATURES + LABEL (horizon_r ≠ grid P&L), not sample size. Reads analysis_data/gridstat_scan_mom snapshot. Run: `python gridstat_meta_xs.py [folds] [trials]`.
├── gridstat_meta.py                 ← AFML meta-labeling experiment (Ch.3.6/4/7/8/14): pooled gold+silver+oil, uniqueness-weighted RF P(win) vs fingerprint gate, purged K-fold + Deflated/Probabilistic Sharpe. Reads analysis_data/ snapshots only. Run: `python gridstat_meta.py [Nfolds] [Ntrials]` (default 6/50). `--write` saves a dated report `analysis_data/meta_report_<date>.txt` + per-symbol EA-readable sizing tables `analysis_data/gridstat_sizing_meta_<sym>_<date>.csv` (rigor format, size_mult col idx 1; size = RELATIVE ML-confidence rank 0.75x→2.0x, negative-R zeroed, thin→0.75x). Tables are RESEARCH artifacts in analysis_data/ — NOT auto-promoted; to A/B copy one → Common\Files\gridstat_sizing_<sym>.csv + set UseSizingTable=true/StatsFilterEnabled=false. OOS verdict: standalone ML does NOT beat the live fingerprint config (per-trade ML edge real but small, lost to turnover; needs more instruments / ONNX-per-trade, not a fingerprint table).

---

## How to Run

```bash
# Install dependencies
pip install -r requirements.txt

# Set MT4 terminal path in config.py first, then:
python hedger_monitor.py
```
